<script>

    function qs(selector) { return document.querySelector(selector); }

    var firstTime = true;
    var startTime = new Date();
    var visibleClassAdded = false;
    var visibleClassRemoved = false;

    function waitForMaavaronClose() {

        // check if the user is paying
        if (CookieUtil.getCookie('HtzPusr') || CookieUtil.getCookie('TmPusr') || CookieUtil.getCookie('HdcPusr')) {
    		document.dispatchEvent(new Event('maavaronClosed'));
            return;
        }

        // hide the page
        if (firstTime) {
    		document.documentElement.style.opacity = 0;
            firstTime = false;
        }

        if (document.body) {

            // get all the elements of the body except the maavaron & outbrain
            var elements = [];
            document.body.childNodes.forEach(function (child) {
      			var isOutbrain = child.tagName && child.tagName.toLowerCase() == 'div' && child.classList.contains("OUTBRAIN");
                if (!child.nodeName.toLocaleLowerCase().match('comment|text|script|style|meta|link|title') && child.getAttribute('id') != 'maavaron' && !isOutbrain) {        			        			          			        			
			        elements.push(child);        			
                }
            });

            // hide the elements
            elements.forEach(function (child) {
      			child.style.opacity = 0;
            });

            var currentTime = new Date();
            var timeElapsed = currentTime - startTime;


            // check if the maavaron is visible
            var maavaronIsVisible = qs('#maavaron') && qs('#maavaron').classList.contains('o-ad--maavaron-is-visible');

            if (!visibleClassAdded && maavaronIsVisible) {
                visibleClassAdded = true;
      			qs('html').style.opacity = null;
                console.log('maavaron is visible: ' + timeElapsed);
            }

            // check if the maavaron was closed
            if (visibleClassAdded && !maavaronIsVisible) {
                visibleClassRemoved = true;
            }

            // check time out
            var timeOut = false;
            if (!visibleClassAdded && timeElapsed > 2000) {
                timeOut = true;
                console.log('maavaron waiting time out: ' + timeElapsed);
                if (qs('#maavaron')) {
                    qs('#maavaron').style.display = 'none';
                }
            }

            // check if the maavaron was closed or the dfp.js tells us to stop waiting or request timeout
            if (visibleClassRemoved || window.stopWaitingForMaavaron || timeOut) {
                console.log('maavaron was closed: ' + timeElapsed);                
    			document.documentElement.style.opacity = null;      			
                elements.forEach(function (child) {
                    if (!child.dataset.hidden) {
      					child.style.opacity = null;
                    }
                });
      			document.dispatchEvent(new Event('maavaronClosed'));
                return;
            }

        }

        setTimeout(function () {
            waitForMaavaronClose();
        }, 100);
    }


    waitForMaavaronClose();

</script>

<script>
    function initDfpScript() {
        var dfpConfig = {};
      if (dfpConfig) {
          window.q = new DFP.default(dfpConfig);
          window.q.initGoogleTag()
                  .then(function googleTagReady() {
                      if (typeof AdBlockUtil !== 'undefined' && typeof AdBlockUtil.killadblock === "function") {
                          window.addEventListener('adblockDetected', AdBlockUtil.killadblock);
                      }
                      googletag.pubads().collapseEmptyDivs();
                      if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function'){
                          q.adManager.showAllSlots();
                      }
                  })
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part I: dfpConfig is not ready!");
      }
        if (dfpConfig) {
          window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function loadAllAds() {
              window.q.initGoogleTag().then(function googleTagReady() {
				  if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function'){
                      q.adManager.showAllSlots();
				  }
              })
          })
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part II: dfpConfig is not ready!");
      }
        if (dfpConfig) {
          window.addEventListener('load', function loadAllAds() {
              setTimeout(function() {
                  window.q.initGoogleTag().then(function googleTagReady() {
                    if(typeof q.adManager.showAllSlots === 'function') {
						q.adManager.showAllSlots();
					}
                  });
              }, 10000);
          });
      }
      else {
          throw new Error("Init Part III: dfpConfig is not ready!");

  }

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",function(){
function parseDate (date) {
        var parsedDate = date.split(' ');
        var tz = parsedDate[4];
        var formattedTz = 'UTC+0';
        switch (tz) {
            case 'IST':
                formattedTz = 'UTC+2';
                break;
            case 'IDT':
                formattedTz = 'UTC+3';
                break;
            default:
                formattedTz = 'UTC+0';
                break;
        }

        var currentDate = new Date(parsedDate[0] + ' ' +  parsedDate[1] + ' ' +  parsedDate[2] + ' ' +  parsedDate[3] + ' ' +  parsedDate[5] + ' ' +  formattedTz);

        var tzoffset = (currentDate).getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
        return new Date(currentDate.getTime() - tzoffset).toISOString()
    }
    function pdate() {
        try {
            var nodes = document.querySelectorAll('meta[property="vr:published_time"]');
            return parseDate(nodes[0].content);
        } catch (e) {
            return null
        }
    }

<script type="text/javascript">

function getCookie(){
	var cookieName = arguments[0];
	var cookieValue = null;
	if (document.cookie && document.cookie != '') {
		var cookies = document.cookie.split(';');
		for (var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
			var cookie = (cookies[i]).trim();			
			// Does this cookie string begin with the name we want?
			if (cookie.substring(0, cookieName.length + 1) == (cookieName + '=')) {
				cookieValue = unescape(cookie.substring(cookieName.length + 1));
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	return cookieValue;
}


var Pushwoosh = Pushwoosh || [];
var userCookie = getCookie("tmsso");
var ssoiId;
var userType = "anonymous";
if (userCookie == undefined){
	ssoId =  null;
} else {
 	ssoId =  userCookie["userId"];
  	if (getCookie("HtzPusr") == undefined){ 
    	userType = "registered";
    } else {
    	userType = "payer";
  	}
}

        'UserType': userType

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    // Define gemius
    var gemius_identifier = 'BxXr4O8igCJS3C8Igq6EJcdRfUpwfUeYD_H2p9dowH3.L7';

    function gemiusClickMoreFromIr() {
        gemiusBase(['click_tm_hp=more_from_ir']);
    }

    function gemiusClickListTitle() {
        gemiusBase(['click_tm_hp=ir_title']);
    }

    function gemiusClickTeaser(elem, elemIndex) {
        var paramName;
        switch (elemIndex) {
            case 0: paramName = 'click_tm_hp_top_right'; break;
            case 1: paramName = 'click_tm_hp_top_left'; break;
            case 2: paramName = 'click_tm_hp_bottom_right'; break;
            case 3: paramName = 'click_tm_hp_bottom_left'; break;
        }
        if (paramName) {
            var companyName = elem.getElementsByTagName('span')[1].textContent;
            var articleTitle = elem.getElementsByTagName('span')[2].textContent;
            return function() {
        		gemiusBase([
                	paramName + "=article",
                	'company=' + companyName,
                	'article_title=' + articleTitle
            	]);
      		};
        }
    }

    function gemiusBase(params) {
        if (window.gemius_event) {
            params.unshift('_action_', gemius_identifier);
            gemius_event.apply(window, params);
        }
    }

    function loadGemiusScript() {
        if (!window.gemius_event) {
            var fileref = document.createElement("script");
            fileref.setAttribute("type", "text/javascript");
            fileref.setAttribute("src", "//www.themarker.com/st/media/smadar/gemius.js");
            if (typeof fileref != "undefined") {
                document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(fileref)
            }
        }
    }
    loadGemiusScript();

    //  Bind events to elements
    var moreFromIR = document.querySelector('aside.l-sidebar div.media__content a[data-section="ir"]');
    if(moreFromIR) {
      moreFromIR.onclick = gemiusClickMoreFromIr;
    }
    var irListTitle = document.querySelector('aside.l-sidebar div.media__fig div.has-sec-head a');
    if (irListTitle) {
        irListTitle.onclick = gemiusClickListTitle;
    };
    var teasersArray = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('aside.l-sidebar section[data-back="lst--tokyo-drift"] .js-listitems article'));
    teasersArray.forEach(function (teaser, teaserIndex) {
        teaser.onclick = gemiusClickTeaser(teaser, teaserIndex);
    });
});
</script>

<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
  (function(){
    //var articleId = location.href.match(/1\.\d+/);
    //var isPremiumArticle = location.href.match(/\.premium-/);
  	//var queryString = location.search;

    var userAgent = navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    var isMobile = !!(userAgent.match(/iphone|blackberry|FBAN|android.*mobile/i));

    if ( isMobile && document.cookie.indexOf("doNotRedirectToMobile=") < 0 && location.search.indexOf('desktop') < 0) {
    	var url = window.location.href;
    	var site = window.location.origin;
    	var suffixUrl = url.substr(site.length);
        var queryurl = location.href.split('?')[1];
        queryurl = (!!queryurl == false)? "":"&" +queryurl;
	  	var isLegalReferer = !!(document.referrer.match(/twitter|facebook|google|bit\.ly|htz\.li|t\.co/i));
   		var ref= ""
        if (document.referrer != "")
       	 {
      		console.log('referer:' + document.referrer);
      		var queryStringPreffix = location.search.indexOf("?")!=-1 ? "&" : "?" ;
      		ref = queryStringPreffix + "referer="+document.referrer;
    	 }
    	if(isLegalReferer){
			var encryptedId = '';
      		if(suffixUrl.indexOf("?")>-1){
		    	document.location.href = site +"/wwwMobileSite" +suffixUrl +"&v=" +encryptedId + queryurl+ref;
            }else{
		    	document.location.href = site +"/wwwMobileSite" +suffixUrl +"?v=" +encryptedId + queryurl+ref;
            }
    	}else{
	    	document.location.href = site +"/wwwMobileSite" +suffixUrl+ref;
    	}
    }
  })();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
window.onload = function () {
  const uniElem = document.querySelector('.university-logout')
  if (isUniversity()) {
    	showUniLogout(uniElem)
    	registerUniLogoutEventListener(uniElem)
  } else {
    	hideUniLogout(uniElem)
  }
}

function registerUniLogoutEventListener(uniElem) {
  uniElem.addEventListener('click', eventHandler)
}

function eventHandler() {
  const uniElem = document.querySelector('.university-logout')
  uniElem.removeEventListener('click', eventHandler)
  UserUtil.logout(logoutSuccess, ()=>{}, true)
  hideUniLogout(uniElem)
}

function logoutSuccess() {
	CookieUtil.setCookie("acl", "acl", "/", /(.haaretz.co.il|.themarker.com)/.exec(location.origin)[1],new Date(new Date().setFullYear(new Date().getFullYear() + 3)))
  	CookieUtil.setCookie("tmsso", "", "/", null, new Date(Date.now()))
  	window.location = "/misc/login-page"
}

function showUniLogout(uniElem) {
  const promo = document.querySelector('body > header > div.promo-wrapper--b.promo')
  if (promo) {
    hideElement(promo)
  }
  
  const promoB = document.querySelector('#top-prom-ban')
  if (promoB) {
    hideElement(promoB)
  }
  uniElem.classList.remove('h-hidden')
  uniElem.style.display = "block"
}

function showElement(elm) {
	elm.classList.remove('h-hidden')
}

function hideElement(elm) {
	elm.classList.add('h-hidden')
}

function hideUniLogout() {
  const uniElem = document.querySelector('.university-logout')
  hideElement(uniElem)
}

function isUniversity() {
  	const cookies = CookieUtil.getCookieMap()
  	if (cookies === undefined) return false
  
  	const sso = cookies.tmsso
  	const pusr = cookies.HtzPusr || cookies.TmPusr
  	return sso !== undefined
  			&& sso.university == 'true'
  			&& pusr !== undefined
}
</script>

<header>
<h2 class="t-sec-head t-sec-head--a">רשימת קריאה<span class="js-counter" id="readinglist-count"></span></h2>
<button class="h-fl" data-slider="header-reading-list">
<i aria-hidden="true" class="btn--icn--b [ icn icn--brand ]" data-icon="x"></i>
<span class="icn__alt">סגור</span>
</button>
</header>
<div class="mh__drop-down__content g" id="header-reading-list-content">
<div class="g__cell">
<p class="t-body-text t-epsilon">
                                רשימת הקריאה מאפשרת לך לשמור כתבות ולקרוא אותן במועד מאוחר יותר באתר,במובייל או באפליקציה.
                            </p>
<p class="t-body-text">
                                לחיצה על כפתור "שמור", בתחילת הכתבה תוסיף את הכתבה לרשימת הקריאה שלך.
                                <br/> לחיצה על "הסר" תסיר את הכתבה מרשימת הקריאה.
                            </p>
<p class="t-body-text h-login-hidden">
<strong>שים לב:</strong> על מנת להשתמש ברשימת הקריאה, יש להתחבר לאתר.
                            </p>
<div class="h-login-hidden">

<h3 class="h-ellipsis t-milli--3lines h-ellipsis--dark teaser__h--a"><span>המדינה התעוררה: שכר ממוצע של 12 אלף שקל, בלי תואר אקדמי</span></h3>
</a>
</article>
<article class="js-item teaser teaser--a" data-back="teaser" role="option">
<a href="/markerweek/.premium-1.7965151" tabindex="-1">
<figure>
<img alt="היועץ המשפטי לממשלה, אביחי מנדלבליט" class="lazyload" data-src="https://images.haarets.co.il/image/upload/w_1280,h_960,x_0,y_0,c_crop,g_north_west/w_145,h_109,q_auto,c_fill,f_auto/fl_any_format.preserve_transparency.progressive:none/v1570715126/1.7965236.4290975252.jpg" height="" src="https://images.haarets.co.il/image/upload/w_145,h_109,q_auto,c_fill,f_auto/fl_any_format.preserve_transparency.progressive:none/v1504770845/1.2583580.3602856926.png" title="היועץ המשפטי לממשלה, אביחי מנדלבליט - צילום: ניר קידר" width=""/> </figure>
<h3 class="h-ellipsis t-milli--3lines h-ellipsis--dark teaser__h--a"><span>מנדלבליט: "זה לא פשוט להחליט על כתב אישום נגד ראש הממשלה, אתה מבין? הרי יצא תיקו"</span></h3>
</a>
</article>
<article class="js-item teaser teaser--a" data-back="teaser" role="option">
<a href="/allnews/.premium-MAGAZINE-1.7914422" tabindex="-1">

<h3 class="h-ellipsis t-milli--3lines h-ellipsis--dark teaser__h--a"><span>צינורות של כסף: הצעירים חוזרים לקיבוץ שעושה מיליארדים</span></h3>
</a>
</article>
<article class="js-item teaser teaser--a" data-back="teaser" role="option">
<a href="/news/politics/.premium-1.7972481" tabindex="-1">

<h3 class="h-ellipsis t-milli--3lines h-ellipsis--dark teaser__h--a"><span>המקום הראשון בו כחול לבן תגשש כדי להקים ממשלה</span></h3>
</a>

<script>
    (function() {
        var base;
		var rulerDiv = $(".footer-ruler");
        var expandedDiv = rulerDiv.find(".expanded");
        var collapsedDiv = rulerDiv.find(".collapsed");

        var Ruler = {            
			settings: {
                "timeout": "10000",
          		"maxImpression": "1",
				"interval": "3,7,10,12,15",
                "expandedImg": "https://www.themarker.com/st/media/Media18/shivuk/ruller_TM/2/970x150.jpg",
                "collapsedImg": "https://www.themarker.com/st/media/Media18/shivuk/ruller_TM/2/970x60.jpg",
				"link": "https://pubads.g.doubleclick.net/gampad/clk?id=4714140926&iu=/9401/TheMarker.com.ClickTracker/TheMarker.com.ClickTracker.FullBanner.1"
            },
      	    init: function() {
     			   	base = this;
          			if ((UserUtil.getLoginInfo().userType != 'paying') && (window.location.pathname != "/") && (Ruler.shouldbeShownToday())){
					 $(".close--ruler").click(function() {
						Ruler.collapsed();
					});
					$(".close--ruler").hover(function() {
						$(".close--ruler").css('transform', 'rotate(180deg)').css('transition', 'transform 1s');
					});
					rulerDiv.show();
					Ruler.expanded();
					setTimeout(Ruler.collapsed, parseInt(Ruler.settings.timeout));
				}else{
          			var event = new Event('noRulerDetected');
					window.dispatchEvent(event);
        		}
            },
            expanded: function() {
                (rulerDiv[0]).style.height = "150px";
                (expandedDiv[0]).style.height = "150px";
                expandedDiv.html('<a href="'+Ruler.settings.link.replace("pubads.g.doubleclick.net","rmkz.themarker.com") +'"><img alt = "promotional content" title = "רכוש מנוי הארץ" src="' + Ruler.settings.expandedImg + '"/></a>');
            },
            collapsed: function() {
                (expandedDiv[0]).style.height = "0px";
                (collapsedDiv[0]).style.height = "60px";
                (rulerDiv[0]).style.height = "60px";
                $(".close--ruler").hide();
                collapsedDiv.html('<a href="'+Ruler.settings.link.replace("pubads.g.doubleclick.net","rmkz.themarker.com") +'"><img alt = "promotional content" title = "רכוש מנוי הארץ" src="' + Ruler.settings.collapsedImg + '"/></a>');
            },
			shouldbeShownToday: function() {
				var today = (new Date());
				today.setHours(0);
                today.setMinutes(0);
                today.setSeconds(0);
				if (!localStorage.dayInterval){
					var dateTimeInterval = [];
					var dayInterval = Ruler.settings.interval.split(",");	
					$.each(dayInterval, function( index, value ) {
						var nextIntervalDay = new Date();
						nextIntervalDay.setHours(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setMinutes(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setSeconds(0);
						var nextDay = (nextIntervalDay).setDate(today.getDate() + parseInt(value));
						dateTimeInterval.push(nextDay);
					});
					localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval); 
					return true;
				} else {
					var dateTimeInterval =  JSON.parse(localStorage.dayInterval)
					if (dateTimeInterval.length > 0){
						var nextDateTimeInterval = dateTimeInterval[0];
						if (nextDateTimeInterval <= today){
							dateTimeInterval.shift();
							localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval);
							return true;
						}
					} else{
						var nextIntervalDay = new Date();
						nextIntervalDay.setHours(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setMinutes(0);
						nextIntervalDay.setSeconds(0);
						dateTimeInterval.push((nextIntervalDay).setDate(today.getDate() + 1));
						localStorage.dayInterval = JSON.stringify(dateTimeInterval);
						return false;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
		}
		window.addEventListener('noPopupDetected', Ruler.init);
    })();
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
try{
var DeviceUtil = function() {
     function getTimeStamp() {
      var today = new Date();
      var dd = today.getDate();
      var MM = today.getMonth()+1; //January is 0!
      var hh = today.getHours();
      var mm = today.getMinutes();
      var ss = today.getSeconds();
      var yyyy = today.getFullYear();
      if(dd<10){
          dd='0'+dd;
      } 
      if(MM<10){
          MM='0'+MM;
      } 
      if(hh<10){
          hh='0'+hh;
      } 
      if(mm<10){
          mm='0'+mm;
      } 
      var today = dd+'/'+MM+'/'+yyyy + ' ' +hh +":"+mm + ":"+ss ;
      return today;
    }
    function getDeviceType() {
        if (!('ontouchstart' in window)) {
            return 'Desktop';
        } else if (window.innerWidth > 700) {
            return 'Tablet';
        } else if (!!location.pathname.match('app/')) {
            return 'App';
        } else {
            return 'Smartphone';
        }
    }
    function getManufacture() {
        if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('Nexus')) {
            return 'Nexus';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('SM')) {
            return 'Samsung';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('iPhone|iPad')) {
            return 'Apple';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('LG-')) {
            return 'Lg';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('HTC_')) {
            return 'HTC';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('Windows')) {
            return 'Windows';
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('NOKIA')) {
            return 'NOKIA';
        } else {
            return null;
        }
    }
    function getOSVersion() {
        if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('Android \\d+')) {
            return navigator.userAgent.match('Android \\d+')[0];
        } else if (!!navigator.userAgent.match('OS \\w+')) {
            return navigator.userAgent.match('OS \\w+')[0];
        } else {
            return navigator.platform || null;
        }
    }
    return {
        OSVersion: getOSVersion,
        Manufacture: getManufacture,
        DeviceType: getDeviceType,
        getTimeStamp: getTimeStamp
    }

		var $menu = $(".mh__nav");
	// jQuery-menu-aim: <meaningful part of the example>
	// Hook up events to be fired on menu row activation.
	$menu.menuAim({
		activate: activateSubmenu,
		deactivate: deactivateSubmenu,
		submenuDirection: "left"
	});
	// jQuery-menu-aim: </meaningful part of the example>

	// jQuery-menu-aim: the following JS is used to show and hide the submenu
	// contents. Again, this can be done in any number of ways. jQuery-menu-aim
	// doesn't care how you do this, it just fires the activate and deactivate
	// events at the right times so you know when to show and hide your submenus.
	function activateSubmenu(row) {
		var $row = $(row),
			$submenu = $row.find('ul.mh__nav__sub');

		// Show the submenu
		$submenu.css({
			display: "block"
		});
		

		// Keep the currently activated row's highlighted look
		//$row.addClass("maintainHover");
	}

	function deactivateSubmenu(row) {
		var $row = $(row),
			$submenu = $row.find('ul.mh__nav__sub');

		// Hide the submenu and remove the row's highlighted look
		$submenu.css({
			display: "none"
		});
		
		//$row.removeClass("maintainHover");
	}

	// Bootstrap's dropdown menus immediately close on document click.
	// Don't let this event close the menu if a submenu is being clicked.
	// This event propagation control doesn't belong in the menu-aim plugin
	// itself because the plugin is agnostic to bootstrap.
	$(".mh__nav li").click(function(e) {
		e.stopPropagation();
	});
</script>
